Collective processing, however, does not remove multicopy hardness in general. For every fixed local dimension $d\ge2$, we prove the existence, within an explicit phase family, of complete bases whose copy complexity remains unbounded even under collective separable measurements, with a square root of the number of subsystems as lower-bound scale. Thus sample number, spatial measurement power, and coherent access across repeated local inputs are distinct resources in distributed quantum measurement.

</details>


### [110] [Asymmetric quantum error correction efficiently tackles application-specific noise effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07235)
*Abhishek Yadav,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Michael Epping*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子计算机中比特翻转和相位翻转噪声对量子近似优化算法（QAOA）应用于航班登机口分配（FGA）问题性能的不同影响，并据此提出利用非对称纠错码（如量子奇偶校验码QPC）来更高效地对抗噪声，即使在对称噪声模型下也能通过按应用敏感性分配纠错资源获得收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机中的噪声（比特翻转和相位翻转）对算法和应用的影响程度不同，但现有研究往往忽略了这种不对称性。作者希望通过具体案例展示两种噪声对量子优化算法的差异化影响，并利用这一见解设计更高效的纠错策略，避免盲目对称分配纠错资源。

Method: 以QAOA应用于小型飞行-登机口分配问题为案例，比较比特翻转和相位翻转Pauli噪声在层级（layer-level）和门级（gate-level）噪声模型下的影响，并使用两种电路分解方式：基于CNOT的分解和基于原生R_ZZ的分解。通过仿真分析噪声对混合操作、最终测量及电路内传播的不同影响，然后利用非对称量子奇偶校验码（QPC，即9量子比特Shor码的一般化）进行非对称纠错演示，比较非对称码与对称码的纠错效果。

Result: 仿真结果表明比特翻转噪声对量子优化性能的退化影响更大，这种不对称性在层级噪声模型和原生R_ZZ分解仿真中最为明显。较小的非对称QPC码可以达到与较大对称码几乎相同的纠错提升效果，证明纠错资源应按各错误通道对应用的敏感程度分配。

Conclusion: 即使物理噪声模型本身是对称的，由于不同错误类型对具体应用的影响不对称，非对称量子纠错码依然具有实用价值。纠错资源分配应结合错误通道对应用的实际影响强度以及通过校准获得的噪声信息来确定，而非仅依据物理错误率。

Abstract: Noise is a major challenge for current quantum computers. It can be broadly categorized into bit-flip and phase-flip errors. These two types do not necessarily affect the executed algorithm, thus also the application, in the same way. We illustrate this general effect for the example of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) applied to a small instance of the flight-gate assignment (FGA) problem. We compare bit-flip and phase-flip Pauli noise under both layer-level and gate-level noise models, using two circuit decompositions of the same ideal QAOA unitary: a CNOT-based decomposition and a native-$R_{ZZ}$ decomposition. In the simulations, bit-flip noise produces the larger degradation in the performance of the quantum optimization. The asymmetry is most visible in the layer-level and native-$R_{ZZ}$ simulations. We explain this by how the errors affect mixing, final measurements, and how they propagate inside the circuit. We then exploit these insights to tackle noise particularly efficiently using asymmetric error-correcting codes. As an illustration, we use the quantum parity code (QPC), a generalization of the 9-qubit Shor code, and show that a smaller asymmetric code can achieve nearly the same improvement as a larger symmetric choice. This demonstrates that error-correction resources should be assigned not only according to physical error rates, but also according to how strongly each error channel affects the application. As a result, asymmetric quantum error correction proves useful even in cases where the noise model is symmetric. Finally, we discuss how information about the noise obtained through calibration can be exploited in our approach.

</details>


### [111] [Hudson's theorem fails for the SU(1,1) discrete series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07242)
*Chon-Fai Kam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明Hudson定理在弯曲相空间（SU(1,1)正离散序列对应的双叶双曲面上）不成立：Wigner函数非负的纯态集合严格大于Perelomov相干轨道。具体而言，基态与第一激发态的叠加在Bargmann指数k=1时，混合角至24.93°仍保持Wigner正值，且该正性区域具有正体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hudson定理建立了玻色模式中Wigner函数非负性与高斯态之间的等价关系，是将Wigner负性视为非经典性签名的理论基础。本文旨在探索该定理在弯曲相空间（非平坦辛流形）上的推广是否成立，从而检验Wigner负性作为非经典性判据的普适性边界。

Method: 作者针对SU(1,1)的正离散序列（实现于双叶双曲面的上半叶），系统考察Wigner函数非负的纯态集合。具体方法包括：构造基态与第一激发态的叠加态并数值分析其Wigner函数正性随混合角的变化；利用控制正性的二次型的解析分析，证明该二次型在远场（大双曲距离）退化为单一混合角方向，从而获得正性窗口上限为arctan(1/√(2k))。

Result: 研究发现：Wigner正性纯态集合严格大于Perelomov相干轨道，构成具有正体积的容许区域。在k=1时两态叠加的临界混合角为24.93°，最大宽度在远场极限下由arctan(1/√(2k))界定且在实际中不沿两态方向达到，阈值在中间双曲距离处被固定。

Conclusion: Hudson定理在SU(1,1)弯曲相空间上没有直接类比。Wigner正性不能在该几何下等同于相干态（高斯态）性质，这揭示了Wigner负性作为非经典性签名的有效性依赖于相空间的拓扑与几何结构。

Abstract: Hudson's theorem states that a pure state of a bosonic mode has a non-negative Wigner function if and only if it is Gaussian. It underwrites the reading of Wigner negativity as a faithful signature of pure-state non-classicality. We show the statement has no analogue on curved phase space. For the positive discrete series of $SU(1,1)$, realised on the upper sheet of a two-sheeted hyperboloid, the Wigner-positive pure states form a strictly larger set than the Perelomov coherent orbit. Superpositions of the lowest weight state with the first excited state stay positive up to a mixing angle of $24.93^\circ$ at Bargmann index $k=1$, and the admissible set has positive volume, with a maximal width that is not attained in the two-state direction. We show analytically that the quadratic form controlling positivity degenerates in the far field onto a single mixing angle. That degeneracy bounds the window by $\arctan(1/\sqrt{2k})$ and leaves the threshold itself fixed at intermediate hyperbolic distance.

</details>


### [112] [Spectral Criterion for Disorder-Free Localization of Quantum Walks on Hypercube: QBN Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07267)
*Ce Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子伯努利噪声框架下研究了超立方体上自旋耦合量子行走的无序自由局域化现象，推导出该现象发生的充要判据，并在d=4维Grover行走中显式构造了非均匀不动点，证明了无序自由局域化的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序自由局域化这一无需外加无序即可实现的局域化现象的数学条件与构造方法，将其置于量子伯努利噪声框架中加以严格刻画。

Method: 在量子伯努利噪声框架下建立自旋耦合量子行走模型；通过分析约化酉演化的谱子空间，推出无序自由局域化的充要判据；针对d=4维Grover行走，选取距离依赖耦合φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)，在全+1自旋构型中显式构造非均匀不动点。

Result: 得到了无序自由局域化的充要条件：约化酉演化的某个谱子空间包含位置边际概率非均匀的向量。在d=4维Grover行走模型中，显式构造出全+1自旋构型的非均匀不动点，从而证实该模型存在无序自由局域化。

Conclusion: 从理论上完善了无序自由局域化的判别标准，并首次在超立方体自旋耦合量子行走模型中给出了具体的非均匀不动点构造，为该方向的研究提供了奠基性工作。

Abstract: We study disorder-free localization in a spin-coupled quantum walk on the hypercube within the quantum Bernoulli noises framework. We derive a general criterion for disorder-free localization: it occurs if and only if some spectral subspace of a reduced unitary evolution contains a vector whose position marginal probability is non-uniform. For the Grover walk with distance-dependent coupling \(φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)\) in dimension \(d=4\), we explicitly construct a non-uniform fixed point in the all \(+1\) spin configuration. This implies that disorder-free localization occurs in our model.

</details>


### [113] [Symmetry-resolved tree tensor network analysis of Bell-state discrimination with ancilla-assisted passive linear optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07319)
*Anand Kumar,Wojciech Roga,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光子数分辨的树张量网络方法，用于评估递归结构线性光学Bell态判别器中各Bell输入态的光子计数探测支持区域，并据此计算成功判别概率。该方法利用光子数守恒和模式对称性分离大部分Bell态输出，无需逐一枚举所有相关探测模式即可评估剩余支持区域，适用于最多32个光学模式的递归、乘积和非对称辅助态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助光子能够使被动线性光学Bell态判别超越使用真空辅助模式时50%的成功概率上限。然而，对于固定分析器与辅助资源，判别概率由每个Bell输入唯一对应的光子计数模式决定，评估这些探测支持区域在计算上具有挑战性，需要一种高效的数值方法。

Method: 作者发展了光子数分辨的树张量网络方法，用于递归结构化的分析器中评估探测支持区域。该方法首先利用光子数守恒与模式对称性将大部分Bell态输出分离，随后通过树张量网络构造评估剩余支持区域，避免逐一回忆所有相关光子探测模式。文中还针对因子化辅助资源（在分析器两半具有固定光子数并满足所用对称性）推导出精确的组合关系；对于反射不对称配对，成功概率等于相应对称配置的算术平均。

Result: 该方法应用于递归、乘积及不对称辅助态（最多32个光学模式），再现了已知解析结果和文献基准。通过对较小系统的完全枚举、基于permanent的振幅计算以及独立评估，为数值结果提供了额外的交叉验证。

Conclusion: 该树张量网络方法为线性光学Bell态判别中的探测支持评估提供了一种高效且可扩展的工具，同时导出的精确组合关系加深了对对称与不对称辅助资源配置下判别概率的理解。

Abstract: Ancillary photons allow passive linear-optical Bell-state discrimination to exceed the one-half probability of success limit achievable with vacuum auxiliary modes. For a fixed analyzer and ancillary resource, the discrimination probability is determined by the photon-counting patterns that occur uniquely for each Bell input. We develop a photon-number-resolved tree tensor network method for evaluating these detector supports in recursively structured analyzers. Photon-number conservation and mode symmetries separate most of the Bell-state outputs before the remaining detector support is examined. We establish a tree tensor network based construction to evaluate the remaining support without need of recalling all relevant photon detector patterns separately. We apply the method to recursive, product, and asymmetric ancillary states with up to $32$ optical modes and reproduce known analytical and literature benchmarks. For factorized ancillary resources with fixed photon numbers in halves of the analyzer and the symmetry property used in our analysis, we also derive an exact composition relation; for reflected asymmetric pairings the success probability is the arithmetic mean of the corresponding symmetric configurations. The complete enumeration for smaller systems, permanent-based amplitude calculations, and independent evaluations provide additional checks of the numerical results.

</details>


### [114] [Cat-Code-Protected Controlled Quantum Communication via Non-Local CNOT Gates over Star Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07376)
*Subham Das,Abhishek Sharma,Kailash S*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合cat态编码纠错与非局域CNOT门的受控量子通信协议，通过使物理位置量子比特无需通过光纤传输来减少振幅阻尼事件，在50公里距离上实现了显著优于标准协议的保真度，并验证了其对分束器攻击的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控量子通信在光纤网络中实际应用时，振幅阻尼会随距离呈指数级导致保真度退化，严重阻碍了其实用化进程。现有协议缺乏有效的距离可靠性保障机制，需要新的技术方案来克服这一根本性障碍。

Method: 将两种强大技术结合：1) 采用cat态编码进行量子纠错，以保护量子信息免受振幅阻尼影响；2) 使用最优非局域CNOT门实现分布式量子门操作。协议的核心思路是避免物理位置量子比特本身通过光纤传输，从而减少阻尼事件的发生次数。

Result: 通过密度矩阵仿真证明，cat码保护的非局域CNOT协议在50公里处实现了更高的保真度，显著优于标准协议。同时通过CHSH检验验证了协议在cat码纠错条件下仍能抵御针对分布式纠缠资源的分束器攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究证明cat码保护的受控量子通信在当前技术条件下可行，且架构可自然扩展到多控制器场景，为长距离星型量子网络中带纠错的受控量子通信提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Controlled quantum communication enables secure state transfer between a sender and receiver with the assistance of one or more controllers. However, practical implementation over optical fibre networks is severely hindered by amplitude damping, which reduces fidelity exponentially with distance. We address this challenge by combining two powerful techniques: cat-state encoding for error correction and optimal non-local CNOT gates for distributed gate implementation. The protocol eliminates the need for the physical position qubit itself to travel through the optical fibre, reducing damping events. We show, through density-matrix simulations, that our cat-code-protected protocol with a non-local CNOT operation achieves higher fidelity at 50 km, significantly outperforming the standard protocol. We analyse the protocol's security against beam-splitter attacks and show that CHSH tests provide security against beam-splitter attacks on the distributed entanglement resource despite the cat code's error correction. Our results establish that cat-code-protected controlled quantum communication is feasible with current technology and structurally extensible to multiple controllers, providing a theoretical framework for studying error-corrected controlled quantum communication over long-distance star quantum networks.

</details>


### [115] [Multi-copy and Catalytic Superactivation of Genuine Multipartite Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07380)
*Bora Ulu,Mirjam Weilenmann,Nicolas Brunner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，在基于可分离（biseparable）模型定义的多方贝尔非局域性（GMNL）框架中，存在超激活（superactivation）现象：将两份非GMNL的相关性通过局部布线组合后，可得到具有GMNL的相关性，且该现象适用于任意多方及量子可实现相关性，甚至可通过催化协议在单副本层面实现，从而质疑了该GMNL定义的操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMNL旨在刻画多个远距离观测者之间全局贝尔非局域的相关性，其常用定义基于由非信号（non-signalling）相关性构建的二分可分离（biseparable）模型。作者希望检验这一定义的操作意义，探索是否存在超激活效应，即非GMNL的关联通过局部操作组合后能否产生GMNL。

Method: 作者通过局部布线（local wiring）的方式组合两份二分可分离（非GMNL）的相关性，证明所得组合相关性满足GMNL。同时构造了任意多方情形下的示例，并展示了对于量子可实现的（quantum-realizable）相关性也成立。此外，作者借助催化协议（catalytic protocol），仅通过局部布线在单副本层面实现GMNL的超激活。

Result: （1）将两份非GMNL的二分可分离相关性进行局部布线组合后，可以获得GMNL的相关性；（2）该超激活效应对任意数量的参与方均成立；（3）该效应在量子可实现的相关性中也成立；（4）通过催化协议，超激活甚至可以在单副本层面实现。

Conclusion: 超激活效应的存在表明，基于二分可分离模型的GMNL定义在局部布线下并不稳定——非GMNL资源可通过局部操作组建产生GMNL，这动摇了该定义的操作意义，暗示需要重新审视或改进GMNL的可操作量化框架。

Abstract: Genuine multipartite Bell nonlocality (GMNL) aims at capturing correlations between many distant observers that are globally Bell nonlocal. A commonly used definition for GMNL is based on biseparable models constructed from non-signalling correlations. Here we uncover an effect of superactivation of GMNL in this framework. Specifically, by locally wiring together two copies of a biseparable correlation, i.e. not GMNL, one can obtain another correlation that is GMNL. We show that this is possible for any number of parties, and for quantum-realizable correlations. Finally, we show that superactivation of GMNL is also possible at the single-copy level, via a catalytic protocol involving only local wirings. These results question the operational meaning of this definition of GMNL.

</details>


### [116] [Wavelength-Uniform Quantum Algorithms for Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07384)
*Shi Jin,Chuwen Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在全波长范围内高效的量子模拟算法，利用Weyl变量消除半经典极限下的振荡性，结合精确Hermite矩和量子奇异值变换，实现了空间维度上的多项式复杂度，且离散化和查询界限不依赖于波长的负幂次，甚至能在网格不分辨频率的情况下捕获正确的物理可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟在半经典极限下（de Broglie波长远小于特征长度尺度时）解具有高度振荡性，计算成本极高，是该领域的主要挑战之一。

Method: 采用Weyl变量框架使解不再振荡；利用精确Hermite矩处理势函数的多项式分量，利用量子奇异值变换处理势函数的Fourier分量，从而构造出对全波长范围均高效的量子算法。

Result: 算法在空间维度、离散化和查询界限上均实现多项式复杂度，且不含可能小波长的负幂次项；即使空间网格不分辨频率，仍能捕获正确的物理可观测量，突破了Nyquist-Shannon采样定理的限制。

Conclusion: 该量子算法有效克服了半经典量子模拟中振荡解带来的计算瓶颈，实现了全波长范围的高效模拟，从根本上规避了传统采样定理对网格分辨率的要求。

Abstract: One of the main challenges in quantum simulation is the prohibitive cost of computing its solutions in the semi-classical regime, in which the de Broglie wavelength is small compared with the characteristic length scale and the solution is highly oscillatory. This difficulty is overcome by using the Weyl variable, under which the solution is not oscillatory. Furthermore, we use the exact Hermite moments and quantum singular value transformation to treat the polynomial and Fourier components of the potential, resulting in a quantum algorithm efficient for {\it all} ranges of wavelengths. Specifically, it has a {\it polynomial} complexity in spatial dimension, and discretization and query bounds {\it without} negative powers of possibly small wavelength, thus enabling it to capture the correct physical observables even if the spatial grid does not resolve the frequency, hence defying the Nyquist-Shannon sampling theorem.

</details>


### [117] [Unitary equivalence of Schrödinger and Heisenberg pictures survives in nonlinear quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07391)
*Lajos Diósi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在非线性量子力学中，当哈密顿量依赖于瞬时期望值时，薛定谔图像与海森堡图像的等价性依然成立。尽管酉交织算子依赖于初始态，但它在两种图像间保持一致且能够确保相同的物理预言，并通过可解析求解的例子予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性量子力学中，由于缺乏与状态无关的酉传播子，人们常认为薛定谔图像和海森堡图像的等价性会失效。作者旨在澄清这一误解，证明在特定类型的非线性哈密顿量（依赖瞬时期望值）下，两种图像的等价性实际上是可以保持的。

Method: 作者首先针对哈密顿量依赖瞬时期望值的情形，构造了依赖于初始态的酉交织算子（intertwiner），从而在薛定谔和海森堡图像之间建立联系。随后通过三个解析可解的模型加以验证：非线性自旋进动、平均场谐振力，以及具有平均场耦合的局域场论。

Result: 结果表明，即使在高非线性情况下，只要哈密顿量仅通过瞬时期望值依赖状态，海森堡图像仍然可以保持动力学算符的演化描述，且两种图像给出完全一致的物理预言。酉交织算子的初始态依赖性并不破坏两种图像间的物理等价性。

Conclusion: 薛定谔图像与海森堡图像的等价性在依赖瞬时期望值的非线性哈密顿量中依然成立，初始态依赖的酉交织算子不构成障碍。这一结论为非线性量子力学的形式体系提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The equivalence of Schrödinger and Heisenberg pictures is often thought to break down in nonlinear quantum mechanics due to the absence of a state-independent unitary propagator. We show that for Hamiltonians depending on the state through instantaneous expectation values, the equivalence is preserved. While the unitary intertwiner becomes initial-state dependent, it remains well-defined and ensures identical physical predictions in both pictures. We illustrate this with the analytically solvable examples of a nonlinear spin precession and a mean-field harmonic force, as well as in local field theory with mean-field coupling.

</details>


### [118] [Riemannian Optimization for Multi-Player Quantum Games on Product Unitary Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07397)
*Alireza Habibi,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩展EWL(EEWL)多人量子博弈框架，融合混合策略与量子动作，并设计了酉策略矩阵指数算法(USMEA)，一种几何感知的顺序学习算法，用于在量子多智能体决策系统中联合学习局部酉动作及其经典混合概率，并在标准平滑性和步长条件下给出了收敛性分析与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典博弈论中的困境（如囚徒困境）可以通过量子化来化解，EWL量子博弈已被证明能实现更高支付的合作均衡。然而现有EWL框架主要针对双人纯策略场景，缺乏对多人场景、混合策略以及可学习/可优化的量子策略设计方法的系统性研究，这限制了量子博弈理论在工程化多智能体决策系统中的应用。

Method: 本文首先提出了EEWL框架，将EWL量子博弈扩展至多人量子博弈的混合策略场景：每个玩家控制一组酉算子作为量子动作，并对这些动作采用经典混合策略；收益定义为作用在共享量子态上的厄米奖励算符的期望值，该量子态按EEWL协议生成与测量。随后提出USMEA算法，这是一种几何感知的顺序学习算法，在每个玩家联合学习一组可训练的局部酉动作及其相应的经典混合概率，作为多智能体量子决策系统的学习-控制层。算法在标准平滑性和步长条件下进行了收敛性理论分析，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 理论分析证明了USMEA在标准平滑性和步长条件下具有收敛性保证，数值实验验证了算法的有效性，表明经典优化方法可以被系统性地集成到工程化量子策略交互的设计与分析中。

Conclusion: 本文展示了如何将经典优化方法系统性地融入量子博弈策略设计，为多人量子混合策略博弈提供了可学习、可优化的统一框架（EEWL+USMEA），有望成为多智能体量子决策系统的通用学习与控制层。

Abstract: Quantum game theory is an extension of classical game theory that uses quantum principles in game theory. The Eisert-Wilkens-Lewenstein (EWL) quantum game is an early example of the two-player classical Prisoner's Dilemma transformed into a quantum Prisoner's Dilemma. In the EWL game, the players choose pure quantum strategies represented by unitary matrices. This extension can resolve the classical dilemma by enabling cooperative equilibrium with higher payoff. In this paper, we first discuss the Extended EWL (EEWL) for multiplayer quantum games with mixed strategies. In EEWL, each player controls a set of unitary operators as quantum actions and uses a classical mixed strategy over these actions. The payoffs are defined as expectation values of Hermitian reward operators acting on a shared quantum state, which is generated and measured according to the EEWL protocol. We then propose the Unitary Strategy Matrix Exponential Algorithm (USMEA), a geometry-aware sequential algorithm for the EEWL mixed-strategy setting, in which each player jointly learns a trainable set of local unitary actions and the associated classical mixing probabilities. Thereby it acts as a learning-and-control layer for multi-agent quantum decision systems. We analyze the convergence properties of USMEA under standard smoothness and step-size conditions and validate the theory with numerical experiments. These results show how classical optimization methods can be systematically integrated into the design and analysis of engineered quantum strategic interactions.

</details>


### [119] [Photonic Time-Delayed Quantum Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07416)
*Ekaterina Protsenko,Caterina Vigliar,Francesco Da Ros*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并数值模拟了一种基于时间延迟非线性干涉仪中非线性相互作用和光子数分辨探测的光子量子极端学习机（QELM），通过二元分类非线性two moons基准任务验证了其计算能力，并在有损条件下验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种实验上可实现的光子量子极端学习机平台，利用时间延迟非线性干涉仪中的非线性相互作用来构建量子储备池，评估其计算能力和在实际损耗条件下的性能表现。

Method: 采用时间延迟非线性干涉仪作为储备池结构，结合光子数分辨探测进行读出。通过二元分类非线性two moons基准任务评估储备池计算能力。分析方法包括：分析学习到的输出权重、比较不同量子读出方案、测试紧凑特征集（基于光子数关联）对分类精度的影响，以及在有损条件下观察权重重新分布情况。

Result: 基于光子数关联的紧凑特征集能在降低测量复杂度的同时保持分类准确率；在有损耗情况下，QELM通过将学习到的权重自然重新分布到更大范围的特征集合上，维持了分类性能。

Conclusion: 时间延迟光子实现具有对损耗的鲁棒性，该方案是一个实验上易获取的QELM平台，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose and numerically simulate a photonic quantum extreme learning machine (QELM) based on non-linear interactions in a time-delay non-linear interferometer with photon-number-resolving detection. We theoretically demonstrate the reservoir's computational capability by binary classification of the non-linear two moons benchmark. Analysis of the learned output weights and alternative quantum readouts shows that compact feature sets based on photon-number correlations preserve classification accuracy while potentially reducing measurement complexity. We further show that the QELM maintains its classification performance under the effect of losses by naturally redistributing the learned weights across a larger set of available features. These results demonstrate the resilience of the time-delay photonic implementation and highlight its potential as an experimentally accessible QELM platform.

</details>


### [120] [High-purity fluorescence photon bundle emission from two separate emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07454)
*Zhicai Chen,Jun Xu,Deyi Kong,Xiangming Hu,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种从两个空间分离的双能级发射体产生高纯度荧光双光子束的方案，其相关激发由强驱动辅助发射体介导。由于目标发射体的有限激发空间固有地排除了高激发流形，从而消除了与不期望的高激发态相关的杂质光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于玻色子模式的方法难以避免高激发态产生的杂质光子，影响光子束纯度。需要找到一种内在机制来消除高激发流形，从而获得高纯度双光子束。

Method: 利用两个空间分离的双能级发射体作为目标系统，其有限激发空间从本质上排除高激发态；通过强驱动的辅助发射体介导两个目标的关联激发；解析分析非共振单激发态的残余布局，并通过抑制相应泄漏来提高光子束纯度。

Result: 方案实现了高纯度荧光双光子束的产生，有限激发空间消除了高激发流形相关的杂质光子；解析表征了残余的单激发态布局，证明了抑制泄漏可进一步提升光子束纯度。

Conclusion: 该方案为构建高纯度量子光源提供了可行途径，在量子信息处理中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a scheme for generating high-purity fluorescence two-photon bundles from two spatially separate two-level emitters, whose correlated excitation is mediated by a strongly driven auxiliary emitter. Unlike bosonic-mode-based approaches, the finite excitation space of the two target emitters intrinsically excludes higher-excitation manifolds, and thus eliminates impurity photons associated with undesired higher-excitation states. We further analytically characterize the residual population of off-resonant single-excitation states and show that suppressing the corresponding leakage improves photon bundle purity. Our scheme offers a feasible pathway for constructing high-purity quantum light sources with promising applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [121] [Stationary states for a particle in a box with slanted walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07505)
*Nivaldo A. Lemos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了斜壁无限势阱中粒子的量子力学问题。能量本征值由含第一类Airy函数的超越方程确定，并通过图形与数值方法求解。研究发现大量子数下能级渐近趋近，波尔对应原理比标准盒中粒子或谐振子情形更精确地成立。在对称斜壁情形下，超过临界斜率后基态不再是受限程度最小的本征态，这一结果与谐振子行为形成意外对比。该问题还可帮助学生熟悉Airy函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索斜壁无限势阱这一非标准但可解析处理的量子力学模型，研究其能谱渐近行为与态受限性质，特别关注对称斜壁情形下出现的反直觉现象，并为学生提供接触Airy函数的教学机会。

Method: 将斜壁无限势阱的定态薛定谔方程解归结为含第一类Airy函数的超越方程；利用图形法与数值方法求解能量本征值；讨论两个极限情形；通过直觉论证结合Airy函数基本性质证明大量子数下相邻能级间距趋于零的渐近行为；计算部分定态的经典禁戒区概率以量化受限程度，并与谐振子做对比。

Result: 图形分析表明大量子数下相邻能级趋于密集，该渐近行为由Airy函数性质严格确证。由此，波尔对应原理在斜壁势阱中比标准盒中粒子或谐振子情形更精确地成立。对受限程度的计算发现：在对称情形下，当壁斜率超过临界值时，基态不再是受限程度最小的本征态，打破了谐振子中基态始终最不受限的规律。

Conclusion: 斜壁无限势阱展现出丰富的物理特性，包括能谱渐近行为、对应原理的精确实现以及对称情形下基态受限状态的异常转变。该问题兼具物理趣味性与教学价值，可作为物理本科生接触Airy函数、深化量子力学理解的理想素材。

Abstract: The quantum mechanical problem of a particle in a infinite potential well with slanted walls is studied. The energy eigenvalues are determined by a transcendental equation involving the Airy function of the first kind. Two limiting cases are discussed. Next, the allowed energies are found by graphical and numerical methods. The graphical analysis hints that for large quantum numbers the consecutive energy levels get arbitrarily close together. This asymptotic behavior of the energy spectrum actually holds, as shown by means of an intuitive argument and confirmed by resorting to basic properties of Airy functions. In a sense, Bohr's correspondence principle is more accurately fulfilled than in the cases of the standard particle in a box or the harmonic oscillator. A state is regarded the more confined the less the probability of finding the particle in the classically forbidden region. For some stationary states the confinement degree is calculated and a comparison is made with the harmonic oscillator, for which the ground state is the least confined of all stationary states. In the symmetric case --- right and left walls of equal slope --- something unexpected happens: beyond a certain critical slope the ground state is no longer the least confined eigenstate. Over and above its interesting physical features, this problem provides students with the opportunity to get acquainted with Airy functions, which do not belong to the standard mathematical repertoire of physics undergraduates.

</details>


### [122] [Achieving the Quantum Limits with Twin-Field Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07517)
*Yaohua Li,Fan Yang,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种孪生场传感器（TFS）方案，通过对原始传感器的信号及其系统副本进行联合测量，使光子计数或零差检测能够饱和量子Fisher信息界，并在多种物理实现（从两能级原子到混合量子Rabi模型）中均达到理论量子极限，且可同时联合传感多对参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续测量在量子实验中起着基础性作用，但如何从连续探针信号中理解和提取最大可能的信息仍然是一个重要挑战。作者的目标是找到一种通用方法，使实际可实现的简单测量能够饱和量子Fisher信息所给出的理论极限。

Method: 作者展示了对于一大类传感器，可以通过对来自原始传感器和合适的系统副本的信号进行联合测量来饱和量子Fisher信息界。该方法涵盖多种实现场景，包括两能级原子模型和混合量子Rabi模型，并以量子Fisher信息矩阵为度量标准来验证参数联合传感的量子极限可达到性。

Result: 在所有研究的实现场景中，简单的光子计数或零差检测即可饱和理论量子极限。此外，作者证明了孪生场传感器（TFS）在面对成对参数联合传感时，也能达到由量子Fisher信息矩阵所量化的量子极限。

Conclusion: 孪生场传感方案提供了一种通用且实用的手段，使简单测量策略能够逼近量子力学允许的理论极限，涵盖单参数和多参数联合传感场景，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Continuous measurements play a fundamental role in quantum experiments, yet understanding and extracting the maximal possible information in a continuous probe signal remains a significant challenge. In this work, we show that for a wide class of sensors, the quantum Fisher information bound can be saturated through a combined measurement of signals from the original sensor and a suitable system copy. We elucidate the physical mechanism of such a twin-field sensor (TFS) and study its performance across diverse implementations, ranging from two-level atoms to a hybrid quantum Rabi model. In all of these cases, we find that simple photon counting or homodyne detection saturates the theoretical quantum limit. We further prove that the TFS achieves the quantum limits for the joint sensing of pairs of parameters as quantified by the quantum Fisher information matrix.

</details>


### [123] [Toward Fault-Tolerant Variational Optimization: QAOA under [[4,2,2]] Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07537)
*Matteo Robert Child,Emanuele Dri,Giacomo Vitali,Chiara Vercellino,Alberto Leporati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于[[4,2,2]]纠错码的部分容错QAOA实现，针对方形图上的Max-Cut问题，引入了一个新颖的辅助量子比特介导的逻辑R_ZZ门，使得不同[[4,2,2]]码块中的量子比特能够相互作用。在五种噪声模型、两种连接拓扑（全连接和网格路由）下，使用Cirq和qsimcirq框架进行并行CPU评估。后选择（post-selection）稳定子测量始终能提升最优比特串的采样概率，五次测量收益最强，支持纠错检测作为近期变分量子算法的实用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子近似优化算法（QAOA）在近期含噪声量子设备上表现受限，完全容错量子计算在实际硬件中尚不可行。因此，作者探索部分容错方案，利用轻量级[[4,2,2]]纠错码来检测错误并通过后选择缓解噪声影响，从而在现有硬件条件下提高QAOA输出质量。

Method: 采用[[4,2,2]]错误检测码编码QAOA电路，核心创新在于设计了一种辅助量子比特介导的逻辑R_ZZ门，实现不同编码块之间的量子比特交互。通过稳定子测量进行错误检测和后选择，并在五种噪声模型及两种连接路由（all-to-all与grid-routed）下，使用Cirq/qsimcirq框架配合并行CPU执行进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，对稳定子测量进行后选择能够一致性地改善最优比特串的采样概率，且执行五次稳定子测量时可获得最强的性能提升。编码方案在所有评估的噪声模型和连接拓扑下均优于未编码电路。

Conclusion: 错误检测与后选择相结合的策略在近期量子硬件上是提升变分量子算法质量的实用可行方案。基于[[4,2,2]]码的部分容错QAOA在Max-Cut问题上展现出一致改进，为向完全容错量子计算过渡提供了有价值的中期路径。

Abstract: We present a partially fault-tolerant implementation of QAOA based on the $[[4,2,2]]$ error-detection code, targeting the Max-Cut problem on a square graph. Our main contribution is a novel ancilla-mediated logical $R_{ZZ}$ gate enabling interactions between qubits in different $[[4,2,2]]$ blocks. We evaluate unencoded and encoded circuits under five noise models, with both all-to-all and grid-routed connectivity, using the Cirq and qsimcirq frameworks with parallel CPU execution. Post-selection on stabilizer measurements consistently improves the probability of sampling optimal bitstrings, with five measurements providing the strongest benefit. These results support error-detection as a practical near-term strategy for improving the quality of variational quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [124] [Discrete Dirac equation on quantum graphs: A model of a Dirac particle in a branched lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07539)
*Mashrab Akramov,Carsten Trunk,Davron Matrasulov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维定态离散Dirac方程在具有离散边的图（图网络）上的性质，重点分析了三边星形图这一简单示例，导出了本征态和能谱的精确解，并与连续情形对比，结果吻合良好，为纳米结构和量子器件中相对论量子输运分析提供了系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分析分支结构（如纳米结构和量子器件）上的相对论量子输运，需要建立系统性的数学模型；连续和离散两种图表述的综合研究目前尚不充分。

Method: 针对一维定态离散Dirac方程，在具有离散边的图上同时考虑连续与离散两种图论形式；以三条边构成的星形图作为简单实例，推导本征态与能谱的精确解。

Result: 获得了离散图上的精确本征态与能谱解，与连续情形进行数值对比，表列结果表明两者具有良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为分析分支结构上的相对论量子输运提供了系统且可操作的框架，适用于纳米结构和量子器件的设计场景。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional time-independent discrete Dirac equation on graphs with discrete edges. Both continuous and discrete formulations of the graphs are considered, with particular focus on star graphs with three edges as a simple example. Exact solutions for eigenstates and spectra are derived and compared with the continuum case, showing good agreement in tabulated results. This approach offers a systematic framework for analyzing relativistic quantum transport on branched structures relevant to nanostructures and quantum device design.

</details>


### [125] [Geometry Dependence of Error Thresholds in Two-Dimensional Toric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07579)
*D. Lessing,A. Langheld,C. Krämer,J. A. Koziol,K. P. Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过复制交换蒙特卡洛模拟计算了二维环面码在Nishimori线上的最大似然（最优）纠错阈值，发现在正方形、蜂窝、三角、骰子及kagome五种格子上，对偶格点对的阈值表现出围绕自对偶方格子的特征性分裂，且除平均配位数外，顶点与plaquette的具体排布也对阈值有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对一般晶格几何在次优解码器（如最下权重完美匹配）下的阈值已有研究，但近最优解码器的快速发展使得解析最大似然解码容量（即理论最优阈值）变得迫切且重要，因此需要在不同晶格上精确计算这些最优误差阈值。

Method: 将存在比特翻转或相位翻转错误的环面码映射为二维随机键伊辛模型（RBIM），利用复制交换蒙特卡洛（replica-exchange Monte Carlo）模拟确定RBIM的临界行为，从而在Nishimori线上计算正方形、蜂窝、三角、骰子和kagome五种晶格的最优（最大似然）误差阈值。

Result: 由于格点对偶性，给定晶格上的相位翻转阈值等价于其对偶晶格上的比特翻转阈值。对偶格点对的优化阈值围绕自对偶的方格阈值呈现出特征性的分裂，与次优解码器观察到的定性行为一致。平均配位数对阈值影响最大，但顶点与plaquette的具体排布也对阈值的精确数值起着重要作用。

Conclusion: 环面码的最优误差阈值不仅取决于晶格的平均配位数，还受具体晶格几何结构（顶点和plaquette的排列方式）的显著影响；格点对偶性提供了一种联系不同晶格相位翻转与比特翻转阈值的对称关系。

Abstract: We investigate how the lattice geometry influences the error thresholds of two-dimensional toric codes. In the presence of bit- or phase-flip errors, the toric code maps onto the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model (RBIM). We determine the critical behaviour of the RBIM using replica-exchange Monte Carlo simulations. While previous studies have explored thresholds for general lattice geometries under suboptimal decoders such as minimum-weight perfect matching, the rapid development of near-optimal decoders makes resolving the ultimate, maximum-likelihood code capacity relevant. We compute these optimal error thresholds on the Nishimori line for the square, honeycomb, triangular, dice, and kagome lattices. Owing to lattice duality, the phase-flip threshold on a given lattice is equivalent to the bit-flip threshold on its dual. We find that the optimal thresholds of dual-lattice pairs display a characteristic duality-driven splitting around the self-dual square-lattice, mirroring the qualitative behaviour observed for suboptimal decoders. While the average coordination number has the strongest impact on the error threshold, our results demonstrate that the detailed arrangement of vertices and plaquettes also plays a significant role in determining its precise value.

</details>


### [126] [Topology Obstructs Pure Foundation Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07591)
*Timothy Heightman,Elena Orlova,Philip Mantrov,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个"不可行"定理：对于具有非平凡基态丛（ground-state bundle）的gapped哈密顿量族，任何连续归一化的纯态神经网络波函数表示都必然在某些参数点上与真实基态零保真度，能量偏差至少为一个谱隙Δ。文章进一步证明该结论在简并基态流形、时间动力学和具有混合时空拓扑的周期系统中也成立，并提出算子值模型（operator-valued models）可以在拓扑上规避这一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自旋1/2体系的基态基础模型（foundation models）几乎都是纯态表示，通过条件化哈密顿量参数并用变分原理进行蒙特卡洛采样来估计能量。作者旨在揭示这种纯态表示存在根本性的拓扑障碍，从而指出结构必要性：纯态表示无法无损地表征所有gapped哈密顿量族的基态。

Method: 作者从拓扑学的角度进行分析，利用基态丛（ground-state bundle）的拓扑分类来证明：对于任何gapped哈密顿量族，如果其基态丛是非平凡的，则不存在连续的归一化状态矢量（纯态）模型能够对所有参数值都忠实逼近真实基态。他们通过构造证明给出此障碍的一充分性不成立结论也适用于简并基态流形、含时动力学和具有混合时空拓扑的周期系统，并在单量子比特和双量子比特系统中给出了具体演示。最后论证算子值模型（如密度算子或投影算子表示）在拓扑上天然可以规避该障碍。

Result: 作者证明纯态表示在非平凡基态丛的哈密顿量族上存在拓扑受阻：某参数点处模型与真实基态保真度为零，能量误差至少为Δ，且在一个开邻域内存在O(Δ)的能隙偏差。该结构是拓朴本质的，且在简并流形、含时和周期系统情形下同样成立。造成的后果是保真度磁化率（fidelity susceptibility）出现尖峰，产生假性相变迹象。算子值模型则能拓扑避免此障碍并保留拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 纯态神经网络量子态在表示gapped哈密顿量族基态时存在拓扑层面的结构性限制，无法对所有参数值保持高保真度；这暗示基础神经量子态应采用算子值（operator-valued）表示而非纯态表示，以保证拓扑信息的完整性和表示的一致性。

Abstract: Foundation models for ground states in spin-1/2 systems are a promising method for problems ranging from quantum chemistry to identifying new phase diagrams. Nearly all such models are currently pure-states that condition on the Hamiltonian's parameters, whose Monte Carlo samples give energy estimates according to the variational principle. In this contribution, we show that this representation is topologically obstructed. For any gapped Hamiltonian family whose ground-state bundle is non-trivial, every continuous normalized state-vector model has zero fidelity with the ground state at some parameter value in the Hamiltonian family. For that value, the energy is at least one spectral gap, $Δ$, with an $O(Δ)$ gap in an open-neighbourhood of that point. We show that this is a sufficient no-go also in the case of degenerate ground-state manifolds, time dynamics, and periodic systems with mixed space-time topology, demonstrating these obstructions on one- and two-qubit systems. We discuss how this causes a spike in the fidelity susceptibility, giving a numerical signature of a phase-transition where there is none. We then show that operator-valued models canonically avoid these obstructions and preserve topological information, implying a structural necessity in representation for foundation neural quantum states.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Approximate Counting with Bernoulli Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07604)
*Chengshen Gao,Yongzhen Xu,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带伯努利预言机的量子计数问题，即在元素标签本身具有概率性的情况下，如何估计正分布的比例ρ。作者给出了上界Õ(√ρ/(Δε)+1/(Δ√ε))的查询复杂度，实现了相比经典算法的二次加速，并通过新的量子对手方法组合定理证明了近匹配的下界Ω(√ρ/(Δε))。对于常数间隙Δ=Θ(1)的特例（即有界误差预言机），边界简化为Õ(√ρ/ε+1/√ε)和Ω(√ρ/ε)，完整刻画了查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子计数算法依赖于成员资格预言机，假设每个元素可以被精确标记。然而在实际场景中，标签往往具有固有的概率性（例如伯努利分布），这一假设不成立。因此需要研究在伯努利预言机下（即每个元素对应一个未知偏差的伯努利分布）如何进行量子计数，并分析其查询复杂度是否能保持二次加速。

Method: 算法设计分为两个阶段：(1) 利用量子奇异值变换（QSVT）对偏差间隙进行相干放大，同时保持分布上的叠加不被坍缩；(2) 应用两阶段自适应幅度估计来估计正分布的比例ρ。下界方面，作者通过在布尔-平均情形方向上发展了量子对手方法的全新组合定理，构造了近匹配的查询复杂度下界。

Result: 提出了量子计数在伯努利预言机下的上界为Õ(√ρ/(Δε)+1/(Δ√ε))，实现二次加速；并给出近匹配下界Ω(√ρ/(Δε))。对于常数间隙Δ=Θ(1)的特例（对应有界误差预言机），上界为Õ(√ρ/ε+1/√ε)，下界为Ω(√ρ/ε)，完整刻画了该情形下的查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了概率性标签（伯努利预言机）下的量子计数问题，证明量子算法在此设定下仍能保持二次加速优势，并通过新的量子对手方法组合定理证明了上界的基本最优性。这项工作拓展了量子计数算法的适用场景，将其从精确标记推广到了概率性标记的更一般情形。

Abstract: Quantum counting is a fundamental quantum algorithm that estimates the fraction of marked elements using a membership oracle, achieving a quadratic speedup over classical sampling. The membership oracle, however, assumes exact labeling of each element, but this assumption fails when the labels are inherently probabilistic. We study quantum counting with \emph{Bernoulli oracles}, where given $m$ Bernoulli distributions with unknown biases $p_1,\dots,p_m$ and a gap parameter $Δ$, the goal is to estimate the fraction $ρ$ of \emph{positive} distributions ($p_i\ge1/2+Δ$) to within additive error $ε$. We prove an upper bound of $\tilde{O}\!\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε}+\frac{1}{Δ\sqrtε}\big)$ queries, achieving a quadratic speedup over the classical sample complexity. % of $Θ(ρ/Δ^2ε^2)$.
  Our algorithm first uses the Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT) to coherently amplify the bias gap without collapsing the superposition over distributions, and then applies two-stage adaptive amplitude estimation. We complement this upper bound with a near-matching lower bound of $Ω(\sqrtρ/(Δε))$ via a new composition theorem for the quantum adversary method in the Boolean-over-average-case direction. For the special case of a constant gap $Δ=Θ(1)$, which corresponds to the bounded-error oracle where each query returns the correct label with constant probability, our bounds specialize to $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε+\frac{1}{\sqrtε}\big)$ and $Ω(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε)$, thereby characterizing the query complexity of quantum counting with bounded-error oracles.

</details>


### [128] [Experimental realization of quantum-computing-enhanced sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07613)
*Yifang Xu,Hongwei Huang,Yilong Zhou,Yunlai Zhu,Chuanlong Ma,Lida Sun,Lintao Xiao,Ziyue Hua,Weiting Wang,Zijie Chen,Weizhou Cai,Hekang Li,Haohua Wang,Xiaoting Wang,Ming Li,Chang-Ling Zou,Luyan Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在自旋-振荡器架构中实现了量子计算增强的传感：微波腔作为高维量子寄存器，超导量子比特作为传感器，未知信号直接产生相位Oracle操作，实现了玻色子模式中首次Grover搜索，并在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中观察到量子振幅放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法具有计算优势，但将其融入量子传感并把计算优势转化为信息获取优势仍具挑战性。作者希望在量子计算与量子传感之间建立自然的物理接口，使量子算法（如Grover搜索）的计算优势能直接提升传感性能。

Method: 采用自旋-振荡器架构，微波腔作为高维量子寄存器，超导量子比特作为传感器探头。未知信号直接生成相位Oracle操作，将信号编码为Oracle查询，从而将量子算法（特别是Grover搜索和量子振幅放大）应用于传感问题，在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中执行搜索。

Result: 首次在玻色子模式中实验实现了Grover搜索，在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间观察到量子振幅放大。在相同的传感迭代次数下，该协议比经典顺序搜索提取更多信息并能分辨更多频率候选。

Conclusion: 信号驱动的Oracle为利用基于Oracle的量子算法指明了道路，是实现量子计算增强型传感的有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms offer computational advantages, yet incorporating them into quantum sensing and converting these advantages into enhanced information acquisition remains challenging. Here, we realize quantum-computing-enhanced sensing in a spin-oscillator architecture, where a microwave cavity provides a high-dimensional quantum register and a coupled superconducting qubit serves as the sensor. The unknown signal directly generates the phase oracle operation, establishing a natural physical interface between quantum computation and sensing. We experimentally demonstrate the first Grover search in a bosonic mode and observe quantum amplitude amplification in a Hilbert space spanning more than 128 photons. For the same number of sensing iterations, our protocol extracts more information and resolves more frequency candidates than classical sequential search. Our results establish signal-driven oracles as a route for harnessing oracle-based quantum algorithms and realizing quantum-computing-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [129] [Benchmarking dynamical-structure-factor protocols in programmable neutral-atom geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07634)
*Matteo Grotti,Sergi Julià-Farré,Elisa Ercolessi,Alexandre Dauphin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟中性原子量子处理单元，评估测量动力学结构因子（dynamical structure factor）在Ising类自旋系统中的可行性，涵盖一维横场Ising链、二聚化相互作用链以及二维横场Ising模型，并在有限模拟时间、脉冲调制、位置无序和激光噪声等现实条件下分析协议鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学结构因子是凝聚态物理中的核心可观测量，传统上通过非弹性中子散射获取。近年来量子模拟器可提取类似信息，但已有方法主要局限于可积的一维模型。作者希望将这类协议扩展到更广泛的Ising类自旋系统，尤其是经典数值方法难以处理的大尺度二维系统。

Method: 使用数值仿真（emulation）中性原子量子处理单元，在标准一维横场Ising链之外，扩展测试二聚化相互作用的一维链以及二维横场Ising模型中的动力学结构因子测量协议，并在非理想实验条件（有限模拟时长、脉冲调制、位置无序、激光噪声）下检验协议的鲁棒性。

Result: 结果表明中性原子量子模拟器能够为在经典模拟日益困难的新参数区域探测动力学响应函数提供实用路径；协议在多种Ising类自旋系统及现实实验条件下均展现出可行性。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子模拟器为测量动力学结构因子提供了实际可行的技术路线，在经典计算难以企及的体系中能有效获取动力学响应信息，为后续实验实施奠定了基础。

Abstract: The dynamical structure factor is a central observable in condensed-matter physics, providing direct insight into the excitation spectrum and dynamical response of quantum materials. While it is traditionally accessed in real materials through inelastic neutron scattering, recent works have shown that analogous information can be extracted in quantum simulators using suitable dynamical protocols. So far, these approaches have been demonstrated mainly in paradigmatic integrable one-dimensional models. In this work, we use numerical emulations of a neutral-atom quantum processing unit to assess the feasibility of measuring the dynamical structure factor in a broader class of Ising-like spin systems. Beyond the standard one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, we benchmark the protocol in chains with dimerized interactions and in the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, where spectral properties are difficult to access with classical numerical methods at large scales. We further analyze the robustness of the protocol under realistic experimental conditions, including finite simulation times, pulse modulation, positional disorder, and laser noise. Our results show that neutral-atom quantum simulators can provide a practical route to probing dynamical response functions in regimes where classical simulations become increasingly demanding, paving the way toward experimental implementation.

</details>


### [130] [Microwave-Free $^{13}$C Hyperpolarization of Diamond Particles Enabled by Magic Angle Spinning and NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07653)
*Rémi Blinder,Guzel Musabirova,Daehee Kim,Anshuman Nayak,Jason Twamley,Viateschlav N. Agafonov,Raiker Witter,Jörg Matysik,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种无需微波辐射、仅结合光学照明与魔角旋转（MAS）的13C核超极化方案，在0.2-2 μm随机取向金刚石颗粒中实现高达280-411倍的13C极化增强，稳态绝对极化水平超过0.1%，并通过NV-P1-13C三自旋簇模型解释了低磁场下能级反交叉（LAC）附近的核自旋动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微波驱动的动态核极化（DNP）需要微波辐射，对设备要求高且存在技术限制。利用金刚石NV色心的光学泵浦特性实现核超极化，有望提供一种微波-free的替代方案，并为室温下高灵敏、高分辨率NMR的应用奠定基础。

Method: 将光学照明与魔角旋转（MAS）相结合，在7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下对天然13C丰度（1.1%）和20%同位素富集的随机取向金刚石颗粒（0.2-2 μm）进行核极化实验。通过调节MAS频率（0-6 kHz）系统研究极化增强效果，并构建NV-P1-13C三自旋团簇模型描述能级反交叉（LAC）附近的极化转移动力学。

Result: 在7.1 T磁场下，富集样品获得280倍的极化增强，天然丰度样品获得411倍的增强；稳态绝对13C极化水平超过0.1%。该方案有效缓解了NV自旋哈密顿量各向异性的影响，使得超过99.9%的NV取向都能参与极化转移过程。

Conclusion: 该光诱导MAS核超极化方案无需微波，即可在室温下对随机取向金刚石颗粒实现显著的13C超极化，为将核极化从金刚石颗粒转移至外部核、进而应用于室温高灵敏高分辨NMR开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: Nuclear hyperpolarization from optically pumped color centers in solids offers an alternative to conventional microwave-driven dynamic nuclear polarization (DNP). Diamond can host the nitrogen vacancy (NV) center, whose ground spin state can be readily polarized by light at room temperature, making diamond a candidate platform for nuclear hyperpolarization. We report $^{13}{\rm C}$ nuclear hyperpolarization in randomly oriented diamond particles with sizes ranging from 0.2 to 2 $μ$m, both at natural $^{13}{\rm C}$ abundance (1.1 %) and at 20 % isotopic enrichment, at magnetic fields of 7.1 T and 9.4 T. The protocol combines optical illumination with magic angle spinning (MAS) and does not require microwave irradiation. By investigating the nuclear polarization as a function of the MAS frequency between 0 and 6 kHz at the magnetic field of 7.1 T, we find maximum light-induced polarization enhancements of $280$-fold for the isotopically enriched sample and $411$-fold for the natural abundance sample. Under continuous illumination, steady-state absolute $^{13}{\rm C}$ polarization levels above 0.1 % are reached. A model involving optical pumping of NV centers and spin dynamics near level anticrossings (LACs) in three-spin clusters formed by NV, a substitutional nitrogen (P1) and $^{13}{\rm C}$ is used to describe these findings. The protocol strongly mitigates the effect of the anisotropy of the NV spin Hamiltonian, allowing more than $99.9\%$ of NV orientations to participate in the polarization transfer process. These results represent a first step toward transferring nuclear polarization from diamond particles to external nuclei, with potential applications in sensitive and high-resolution NMR at room temperature.

</details>


### [131] [Dynamical Crossover of the Quantum Fisher Information in the Spin-Boson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07674)
*D. Parlato,G. Di Bello,F. Pavan,G. De Filippis,C. A. Perroni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两能级系统与玻色环境耦合时，动态量子Fisher信息对量子比特能隙估计的影响。在可精确求解的零温纯退相欧姆区域，量子Fisher信息表现出耦合依赖的代数行为并存在动态交叉：弱耦合时无界增长，交叉耦合时趋于有限值，强耦合时消失。有限温度、非欧姆谱及能量弛豫都会改变或调控这一交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学中，动态量子Fisher信息决定了对系统参数的估计精度极限。理解耗散环境如何影响量子比特能隙估计的精度边界，对于实际量子传感和量子计量应用至关重要。此前缺乏对动态量子Fisher信息在环境耦合下的系统理解，特别是环境低频结构与渐近计量行为之间的直接联系尚不明确。

Method: 结合精确解析计算（可解纯退相Ohmic模型）与数值受控的矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state, MPS）模拟。研究零温和有限温度下的Ohmic纯退相、零温下非Ohmic（次欧姆/超欧姆）谱，以及零温Ohmic区域中加入振幅阻尼（能量弛豫）通道后对量子Fisher信息的影响。

Result: (1) 零温Ohmic纯退相：长时量子Fisher信息呈耦合依赖代数行为，弱耦合无界增长、交叉耦合处趋近有限渐近值、强耦合消失；(2) 有限温度抑制长时增长并产生有限时间最大值；(3) 零温非Ohmic：次欧姆区量子Fisher信息消失、超欧姆区发散，交叉消失；(4) 有限温度下超欧姆二次谱出现温度依赖的交叉；(5) 引入能量弛豫后，无界长时增长变为与约化相互作用基态相关的有限渐近值。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了环境低频谱结构与动态能隙传感渐近计量行为之间的直接联系，阐明了热涨落和能量弛豫对理想纯退相动态量子Fisher信息交叉的正则化作用。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical quantum Fisher information of a two-level system coupled to a bosonic environment, focusing on the estimation of the qubit gap. We combine analytical calculations with numerically controlled matrix-product-state simulations. In the exactly solvable pure-dephasing Ohmic regime at zero temperature, the long-time quantum Fisher information displays a coupling-dependent algebraic behavior, leading to a dynamical crossover: it grows without bound at weak coupling, approaches a finite asymptotic value at the crossover coupling, and vanishes at strong coupling. At finite temperature, thermal fluctuations suppress the long-time growth and generate a finite-time maximum, whose dependence on the dephasing coupling retains a clear signa- ture of the zero-temperature crossover. We show that this crossover is absent at zero temperature in non-Ohmic baths: the long-time quantum Fisher information vanishes in the sub-Ohmic and diverges in the super-Ohmic regimes. At non-zero temperature instead, the crossover appears for a super-Ohmic quadratic bath and the crossover coupling becomes temperature-dependent. Moreover, in the zero-temperature Ohmic regime, we introduce an additional amplitude-damping system-bath coupling that induces energy relaxation. This relaxation channel replaces the unbounded long-time growth with a finite asymptotic quantum Fisher information associated with the reduced interacting ground state, while a signature of the pure-dephasing crossover persists in the early-time dynamics. These results establish a direct connection between the low-frequency structure of the bath and the asymptotic metrological behavior of dynamical gap sensing, and show how thermal fluctuations and energy relaxation regularize the ideal pure-dephasing dynamical crossover of the quantum Fisher information.

</details>


### [132] [Subsystem self-correction of the GKP qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07702)
*Brian Chung Hang Cheung,Lasse Bjørn Kristensen,Frederik Nathan,Michael Kastoryano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子信息视角出发，基于子系统码分解对理想GKP哈密顿量的自校正行为进行了自包含推导，证明其在模相空间中可实现有效弦张力，从而展现逻辑寿命的阿伦尼乌斯型标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动量子纠错（即自校正）是量子信息科学中的核心目标。近期理论进展表明GKP码可能具备自校正性质，但缺乏从量子信息角度的清晰理解和自包含推导。

Method: 基于子系统码分解框架，分析GKP哈密顿量在物理正交分量q和p耦合下的细致平衡跳变算符，将其分解为仅作用于规范子系统的主导部分和对逻辑量子比特有非平凡作用的边界项；通过吉布斯权重证明边界项在模胞边缘被指数抑制，从而推导出逻辑寿命的阿伦尼乌斯型标度。

Result: GKP哈密顿量在模相空间中实现了有效弦张力，能量代价随误差接近逻辑扇区边界而增加，导致逻辑寿命呈现阿伦尼乌斯型（指数）标度，展现出与自旋系表面码不同的自校正行为机制。

Conclusion: 理想GKP哈密顿量具有自校正特性，其机制源于模相空间中的有效弦张力，与基于自旋的量子存储（如二维表面码）的自校正机制形成对比，为自校正量子存储器的实现提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: Passive quantum error correction, also known as self-correction, is a holy grail in quantum information science. Recent theoretical advances suggest that the Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code can exhibit self-correction properties, positioning it as a candidate for the realization of self-correcting quantum memories. In this article, we provide a self-contained derivation of the self-correcting behavior of the ideal GKP Hamiltonian, manifested in the Arrhenius type scaling of the logical lifetime, from a quantum-information perspective based on the subsystem code decomposition. When coupling through the physical quadrature $q$ and $p$, the detailed-balance jump operators decompose into a dominant part acting only on the gauge subsystem and a boundary term that acts non-trivially on the logical qubit. The boundary term is then exponentially suppressed by the Gibbs weights near the edge of the modular cells. In contrast to spin-based quantum memories such as the two-dimensional surface code, the GKP Hamiltonian realizes an effective string tension in modular phase space, whereby the energy cost increases as an error approaches the boundary of a logical sector.

</details>


### [133] [Single- and Two-Qubit Gates Driven by an Integrated Photonic Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07708)
*Chris Caron,Zhenyu Wei,Andrei Isichenko,David Heim,Meiting Song,Nick Montifiore,Kaikai Liu,Josiah Dill,Daniel J. Blumenthal,Robert J. Niffenegger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了使用与集成线圈谐振器稳定的可见光波长集成布里渊激光器，驱动88Sr+光学量子比特实现相干单量子比特和双量子比特门操作，单量子比特保真度达99.61%，双量子比特纠缠态保真度达92.35%，证明了集成光子激光器可满足捕获离子量子逻辑的相位噪声要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 捕获离子是量子计算的主要技术之一，但其依赖定制化的桌面激光和光学系统，严重阻碍了系统的可扩展性和鲁棒性。尽管集成氮化硅激光器已在量子态制备、测量和光钟跃迁探测中展现能力，但由于相干量子门对激光性能要求更为严苛，此前尚未实现由集成激光源驱动的相干量子门操作。

Method: 使用可见光波长的集成布里渊激光器，经集成线圈谐振器稳定后，驱动88Sr+光学量子比特。采用随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）测量单量子比特门平均保真度，并通过双量子比特Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用产生纠缠Bell态以评估双量子比特门性能。

Result: 单量子比特门在Clifford门上的平均保真度为99.61% ± 0.03%；通过Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用产生的Bell态保真度为92.35% ± 1.50%。量子比特的裸Ramsey相干时间为660 ± 9 μs，经自旋回波延长至1.750 ± 0.033 ms。

Conclusion: 该研究证明集成的可见光窄线宽光子激光器能够满足捕获离子相干量子逻辑所需的相位噪声要求，为在捕获离子量子处理器中实现可扩展的集成光学系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Trapped ions are a leading technology for quantum computing, but their reliance on bespoke tabletop laser and optical systems remains a major obstacle to scaling and robustness. Integrated silicon nitride lasers, compatible with future monolithic integration with surface electrode ion traps, have recently demonstrated frequency-selective qubit state preparation and measurement as well as interrogation of an optical clock transition. However, coherent qubit gates driven by integrated laser sources have not yet been demonstrated because coherent quantum logic imposes substantially more stringent performance requirements on the laser. Here, we use a visible-wavelength integrated Brillouin laser stabilized to an integrated coil resonator to drive coherent single- and two-qubit gates with $^{88}$Sr$^+$ optical qubits. We measure an average single-qubit fidelity of 99.61% $\pm$ 0.03% per Clifford gate with randomized benchmarking and use a two-qubit Mølmer-Sørensen interaction to generate an entangled Bell state with a fidelity of 92.35% $\pm$ 1.50%. The qubit exhibits a bare Ramsey coherence time of 660 $\pm$ 9 $μ$s, extended to 1.750 $\pm$ 0.033 ms by spin echo. These results demonstrate that integrated visible-wavelength narrow-linewidth photonic lasers can meet the phase-noise requirements for coherent trapped ion quantum logic, providing a path for scalable optical systems integrated within trapped ion quantum processors.

</details>


### [134] [Propaq: A Python package for Heisenberg Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07730)
*Hrishikesh Belagali,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Propaq，一个用于经典模拟量子电路的Python包，实现了海森堡传播技术。它与Qiskit和Cirq等主流量子软件包兼容，在性能上达到或超越同类最先进的Heisenberg传播包，并提供多种独特功能，包括任意基下的Heisenberg传播、混合Schrödinger-Heisenberg模拟、自定义截断与噪声模拟方法，以及详细的日志信息用于分析模拟精度、运行时间和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子电路是量子计算研究中的关键工具，而Heisenberg传播方法（在泡利基下更新算符而非态矢量）在某些电路结构下比Schrödinger模拟更高效。然而，现有实现缺乏灵活性，难以在不同基下进行传播，也不支持混合模拟或详细的性能分析，因此需要一个高性能且灵活的Python实现来填补这一空白。

Method: Propaq通过Python实现Heisenberg传播技术，其核心设计包括：兼容Qiskit和Cirq等主流量子软件包的接口；支持任意基下的Heisenberg传播（而不限于标准泡利基）；融合Schrödinger与Heisenberg两种范式的混合模拟；提供自定义截断策略和噪声模拟方案；内置详细的日志记录功能以追踪模拟精度、运行时间和内存开销。文章还阐述了其后端实现细节。

Result: Propaq在性能上与同类最先进的Heisenberg传播包相当或更优，同时提供了这些包所不具备的多种独特功能（任意基传播、混合模拟、自定义截断与噪声、详细日志），使其在模拟和研究场景中具有更广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: Propaq是一个性能优异且灵活的Heisenberg传播Python工具包，既可作为研究量子模拟方法的基础平台，也能为量子电路的高效经典模拟提供实用支持。其与主流框架的兼容性和独特的可扩展特性使其在量子计算研究社区中具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Propaq, a performant and flexible Python package implementing Heisenberg propagation techniques for classically simulating quantum circuits. Propaq is compatible with major quantum software packages like Qiskit and Cirq, and matches or outperforms similar state-of-the-art packages for Heisenberg propagation. Beyond this, Propaq has multiple distinguishing features for simulation and research. These include Heisenberg propagation in any basis, hybrid Schrödinger-Heisenberg simulation, custom truncation and noisy simulation methods, and detailed logging information for analyzing simulation accuracy, runtime, and memory usage. We describe these features, how to use Propaq for research, and its backend implementation.

</details>


### [135] [QROB: Quantifying Realization Overhead in Quantum Compilation via Reverse Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07776)
*Jintao Li,Kaiqi Li,Rui Wang,Yilun Zhao,Kaixuan Huang,Ying Wang,Jialin Zhang,Zheng-An Wang,Xiaoming Sun,Heng Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QROB提出了一种可扩展的反向构造方法，从可直接实现的配置出发反向生成编译实例，保留逆路径作为可行且与编译器无关的参考基准，并为NISQ SWAP路由、容错格点手术调度及量子存储访问调度提供统一评估基板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需要弥合程序的理想交互拓扑与硬件局部性约束之间的差距，但在大规模评估中缺乏经过校准的参考基准来量化实现开销。现有评估缺乏可行、与编译器无关的对照标准。

Method: 提出QROB（反向构造方法）：从可直接实现的（可路由/可调度）配置出发，逆向生成编译实例，保留其逆路径作为可行的、与编译器无关的参考。该方法可扩展到容量受限的量子内存访问调度，并为NISQ SWAP路由和容错格点手术调度提供统一评估基板。

Result: 在9至156量子比特的系统上评估表明：（1）NISQ编译器参考SWAP成本高达24.1倍、容错编译器参考makespan高达7.0倍，揭示现有工具存在显著实现差距；（2）在QROB参考上训练的深度路由模型在84.8%的真实应用电路上优于Qiskit SABRE；（3）在三个156量子比特的IBM Heron-r2处理器上，QROB参考实现的镜像电路存活率中位数是全量Qiskit O3编译的1.65倍，表明缩小算法编译差距可直接转化为物理保真度收益。

Conclusion: QROB不仅作为诊断基准和数据源具有实用价值，而且证明了算法级编译差距的缩小能直接带来硬件层面的物理保真度提升，为量子编译社区提供了可扩展的统一评估范式。

Abstract: Quantum compilation reconciles a program's idealized interaction topology with hardware locality constraints, yet evaluations at scale lack calibrated references for realization overhead. We present QROB, a scalable reverse-construction methodology that generates compilation instances backward from directly realizable configurations, retaining the inverse paths as feasible, compiler-independent references. QROB provides a common evaluation substrate for NISQ SWAP routing and fault-tolerant lattice-surgery scheduling, while extending its reference-preserving principle to capacity-constrained quantum memory-access scheduling.
  Across systems ranging from 9 to 156 qubits, evaluations highlight QROB's utility as both a diagnostic benchmark and a data source. First, for compiler characterization, QROB reveals substantial realization gaps in existing tools, with NISQ compilers incurring up to 24.1x the reference SWAP cost and fault-tolerant compilers requiring up to 7.0x the reference makespan. Second, as a supervision source for data-driven compilation, a router trained on QROB references outperforms Qiskit SABRE on 84.8% of real-world application circuits. Finally, on real hardware, QROB reference realizations achieve a median mirror-circuit survival rate 1.65x that of full Qiskit O3 compilations across three 156-qubit IBM Heron-r2 processors, demonstrating that closing algorithmic compilation gaps translates directly into physical fidelity gains.

</details>


### [136] [Realization of decoherence-induced averaged symmetry-protected topological phases on quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07831)
*Ruizhe Shen,Xue-Jia Yu,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可编程量子处理器上实验实现了去相干诱导的平均对称性保护拓扑（ASPT）序。通过辅助量子比特辅助的量子电路，在一维簇SPT态上工程化子晶格选择性Pauli-Z退相干，观测到对称荷的衰减、通过破坏性SWAP读出测量的Renyi-2弦关联子仍非平庸，以及半链纠缠谱的简并配对保持，确立了构造和探测ASPT序的门控路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SPT相是为纯态和精确对称性定义的，但开放量子系统中退相干会产生混合态系综，此时平均对称性只能在微观轨迹平均后涌现。需要在实验上理解退相干如何在混合态中产生新的拓扑序结构。

Method: 从一维簇SPT态出发，通过辅助量子比特辅助的量子电路工程化子晶格选择性Pauli-Z退相干；采用破坏性SWAP读出技术测量两副本Renyi-2弦关联子；通过对称荷衰减检测对称性转换；分析约化稳定子扇区的谱权重重分布及半链纠缠谱的两重简并配对。

Result: 实验观测到对称荷随退相干衰减，表明对称性发生转换；两副本Renyi-2弦关联子保持非平庸，揭示密度矩阵层次上的ASPT结构；工程化退相干在约化稳定子扇区中重分布谱权重，同时保持半链纠缠谱的特征性两重简并配对。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在门控量子电路上构造和探测ASPT序的可行途径，证明结构化退相干可作为可编程资源来实现混合态拓扑量子物质，扩展了拓扑物性研究从纯态到混合态的边界。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases are conventionally formulated for pure states protected by exact symmetries. In open quantum systems, however, decoherence generates mixed-state ensembles in which average symmetry can instead emerge only after averaging over microscopic trajectories. In this work, we realize decoherence-induced averaged SPT (ASPT) order on programmable quantum processors. Starting from a one-dimensional cluster SPT, we engineer sublattice-selective Pauli-$Z$ dephasing through ancilla-assisted quantum circuits. We observe the resulting symmetry conversion through the decay of the symmetry charge. Moreover, a two-replica Renyi-2 string correlator measured through destructive SWAP readout remains nontrivial, revealing the ASPT structure encoded at the density-matrix level. We further show that the engineered dephasing redistributes spectral weight among reduced-stabilizer sectors while preserving the characteristic twofold pairing of the half-chain entanglement spectrum. These results establish a gate-based route to engineering and probing ASPT order and demonstrate structured decoherence as a programmable resource for realizing mixed-state topological quantum matter.

</details>


### [137] [Restricted typicality in non-equilibrium quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07832)
*Konrad Pawlik,Piotr Sierant,Jakub Zakrzewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明对于具有时间尺度分离、由守恒荷输运主导动力学的量子多体系统，仅通过约束系统中慢模式的极大熵Scrooge系综，即可系统性地恢复被宏观框架丢失的非线性量子态信息（如半链纠缠熵和计算基下的参与熵），从而提出"全局受限典型性"这一新的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述通用量子多体系统的时间演化需要指数级的计算资源，而流体力学和统计力学等宏观框架虽能高效预测局域可观测量的期望值，却无法提供量子态的非线性特征信息。因此，如何在宏观框架下恢复这些被丢失的量子信息是一个根本性挑战。

Method: 作者利用极大熵Scrooge系综，仅用系统的慢模式（守恒荷的输运模式）作为约束条件来构建系综，通过该系综重构全局时间演化态的复杂非线性量子性质，包括半链纠缠熵和计算基下的参与熵。

Result: 对于表现出时间尺度分离、由守恒荷输运主导的系统，仅利用系统的慢模式约束极大熵Scrooge系综，即可准确重构全局时间演化态的复杂非线性量子性质，包括半链纠缠熵和计算基下的参与熵。结果表明水动力学瓶颈系统在希尔伯特空间的动态受限子流形内表现为典型纯态。

Conclusion: 该工作将正则典型性（canonical typicality）范式推广，提出"全局受限典型性"（global restricted typicality）概念：水动力学瓶颈系统在动态受限的希尔伯特空间子流形中表现为典型纯态，从而系统性地恢复了宏观框架丢失的量子态非线性信息。

Abstract: Characterizing the time evolution of generic quantum many-body systems is a fundamental challenge, as representing the exact state requires exponentially scaling computational resources. While hydrodynamics and statistical mechanics successfully simplify this task by predicting the expectation values of local observables, these macroscopic frameworks provide no information about nonlinear characteristics of the quantum state. In this work, we demonstrate that for systems exhibiting a timescale separation, with dynamics governed by the transport of conserved charges, this lost information can be systematically recovered. By constraining the maximum-entropy Scrooge ensemble solely by the system's slow modes, we accurately reconstruct complex, nonlinear quantum properties of the global time-evolved state, including the half-chain entanglement entropy and the participation entropy in the computational basis. Our results generalize the paradigm of canonical typicality, revealing that a hydrodynamically bottlenecked system behaves as a typical pure state within a dynamically restricted submanifold of the Hilbert space - a phenomenon we term global restricted typicality.

</details>


### [138] [Snakes on a Hyper-Plane: Dynamical and Ontological Locality in Everettian Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07860)
*Travis Norsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文质疑新兴共识（即Spacetime State Realism版本的Everettian量子力学与相对论动力学局域性相容），指出该理论中的非局域beables（非局域区域的约化密度矩阵）对现行动力学局域性表述构成挑战，暗示其与相对论因果性可能并不相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界存在一种共识：Wallace和Timpson的Spacetime State Realism版本的Everettian量子力学虽然存在本体论非局域性（不可分离性），但被认为与相对论要求的动力学局域性完全兼容。作者旨在检验这一共识是否成立，即当理论引入非局域beables后，其与相对论因果性的兼容性是否仍能维持。

Method: 作者分析SSR EQM的本体论结构，区分局部区域约化密度矩阵（RDMs）与非局部区域的RDMs。前者不受到过去光锥外干预的影响，构成所谓"动力学局域性"的证据；但后者作为非局域beables被理论额外引入。作者考察这些非局域beables对现行动力学局域性表述（如Wallace-Timpson方案）可能造成的理论困难。

Result: 作者发现，非局域beables（非局域区域的RDMs）的存在使现行动力学局域性的表述方式面临实质性的复杂化问题，并对SSR EQM与相对论因果性相容的共识结论提出至少是暗示性的质疑。

Conclusion: 与新兴共识相反，SSR EQM宣称的与相对论因果性的兼容性是有问题的；非局域beables的引入对动力学局域性的形式化表述构成棘手困难，因此该理论是否真正与相对论因果性相容是需要重新审视的开放问题。

Abstract: An apparently emerging consensus holds that Everettian Quantum Mechanics (EQM), in the Spacetime State Realism (SSR) version of Wallace and Timpson, involves ontological non-locality (i.e., non-separability) but is fully compatible with the dynamical notion of locality usually taken to be required by relativity. The claim of dynamical locality is based on the fact that the local beables postulated by the theory (namely, Reduced Density Matrices, RDMs, associated with localized regions) are not affected by interventions outside their past light cones. However, the theory's non-separability means that it posits non-local beables (namely RDMs for non-localized regions) in addition to the local ones. We argue here that the involvement of non-local beables introduces complications for extant formulations of dynamical locality and at least suggests, contrary to the emerging consensus, that SSR EQM should perhaps not be considered compatible with relativistic causality after all.

</details>


### [139] [Quantifying Margenau--Hill Nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07869)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Margenau-Hill准概率（MHQ）分布的矩来量化量子非经典性，提出了对数MHQ负性作为非经典性量化指标，并推导了基于低阶矩的严格下界层次结构，其中四阶矩界限在偶次矩中最强。对于量子比特系统，导出了精确MHQ负性的紧上界及对应的极值态，并给出了任意量子比特可观测对基于四阶矩的最优正性阈值。相关矩可通过干涉测量或经典阴影技术进行多拷贝表示与估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准概率分布是描述量子系统非经典特征的重要工具，但完整重建准概率分布需要大量测量资源。作者希望在不重建整个分布的情况下，仅通过低阶矩来量化基于MHQ分布的非经典性，以建立更高效的量化框架。

Method: 本文首先引入对数MHQ负性作为非经典性量化指标，然后推导由低阶矩构成的严格下界层次结构（以矩为变量的不等式体系）。在此基础上，针对量子比特系统推导精确MHQ负性的紧上界并寻找极值态，同时利用优化方法确定基于四阶矩的正性阈值。最后展示了相关矩可通过多拷贝测量方案（干涉测量和经典阴影技术）实现。

Result: 建立了矩的层次下界，其中四阶矩界限在偶次矩相关界限中最优最强；对量子比特给出了MHQ负性的紧上界及对应极值态；确定了任意量子比特可观测对的四阶矩正性最优阈值；证明了相关矩具有确切的多拷贝表示，可通过干涉测量或经典阴影技术进行估计。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于矩的框架，能够仅从有限数量的低阶可观测量中提取有关MHQ负性的定量信息，为非经典性的高效量化提供了实用途径，避免了完整重建准概率分布的需求。

Abstract: Quasiprobability distributions offer a useful way of describing nonclassical features of quantum systems through their departure from classical probability theory. In this work, we investigate the quantification of nonclassicality associated with the Margenau--Hill quasiprobability (MHQ) distribution using its moments, without requiring reconstruction of the full distribution. First we introduce the logarithmic MHQ negativity as a quantifier of nonclassicality, and then derive a hierarchy of rigorous lower bounds in terms of low-order moments. Within the resulting hierarchy, the fourth-moment bound is the strongest among the bounds based on even moments. For qubits, we further derive a tight upper bound on the exact MHQ negativity and identify the corresponding extremal state. We also obtain an optimal positivity threshold based on the fourth moment for arbitrary pairs of qubit observables. Finally, we show that the relevant moments admit exact multicopy representations, providing a route to their estimation using interferometry or classical shadow techniques. Our results establish a moment-based framework for extracting quantitative information about MHQ negativity from a finite set of low-order observables.

</details>


### [140] [Measurement-induced remote activation of nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07875)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非经典性（通过准概率分布非正性定义）可以被远程激活：关联伙伴的局部测量即可将约化态为经典的一方驱动到真正的非经典条件态。论文针对两量子比特系统推导了最大单分支效应及其概率加权平均的闭式解，并证明了共享态无局部偏置时该探测在一切测量中为最优。非幺正、与该方轴向错位的局部噪声可增强效应，而任何幺正信道均无帮助。该非经典性还可解锁同一参考可观测量的反常弱值，并通过可精确求解的混合量子比特-谐振子模型展示了相同机制产生Wigner负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非经典性是否可以远程激活，即在关联伙伴仅施加局部测量的条件下，使原本经典的一方产生真非经典态，从而确立测量诱导的操控作为远程激活非经典性的操作资源。

Method: 针对两量子比特系统推导最大单分支效应及概率加权平均的闭式表达式；证明在共享态无局部偏置时两结果投影测量对所有观测为最优；分析局部噪声（非幺正且错位的信道可增强，幺正信道无帮助）；利用混合量子比特-谐振子模型验证机制可推广至Wigner负性。

Result: 获得了远程激活非经典性的闭式结果，确立了最优测量形式，发现了非幺正且错位噪声可增强而幺正信道无帮助的规律，并证明该机制同时解锁反常弱值并生成Wigner负性。

Conclusion: 测量诱导的操控（steering）是远程激活非经典性的操作性资源，为无需直接访问目标量子系统即可生成非经典资源提供了理论方案。

Abstract: Nonclassicality, defined through nonpositivity of quasiprobability distributions such as the Wigner and Kirkwood-Dirac distributions, underlies key quantum advantages. We show that it can be remotely activated: local measurements by a correlated partner alone can drive a party whose reduced state is classical into a genuinely nonclassical conditional state. For two-qubit systems we obtain closed forms for the maximal single-branch effect and for its probability-weighted average under the best two-outcome projective measurement, the latter proven optimal over all measurements when the shared state carries no local bias. Local noise on the steered party, evaluated relative to its own noise-updated reference basis, can enhance the effect when non-unital and misaligned with that party's axis, whereas no unital channel ever helps. This nonclassicality is shown to unlock, for the same reference observable, anomalous weak values unavailable to the unsteered state under arbitrary postselection. An exactly solvable hybrid qubit-oscillator model shows the same mechanism generates Wigner negativity. These results identify measurement-induced steering as an operational resource for remotely activating nonclassicality.

</details>


### [141] [Riemannian optimization for linear optical problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07890)
*Pablo V. Parellada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了定义在多端口干涉仪酉矩阵上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式，并利用黎曼优化算法（如梯度下降或BFGS）寻找干涉仪的最优配置，应用于线性光学态制备优化，显著提升了NOON态和光子催化制备的成功概率，并比现有方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学量子科学中的许多应用需要在多端口干涉仪上搜索某种最优配置（例如，寻找隐伏态制备时最大化保真度和成功概率）。已有的优化方法计算效率较低，难以处理涉及更多模式和光子的线性光学问题。

Method: 推导了定义在干涉仪酉矩阵（即黎曼流形上的点）上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式；将该梯度应用于黎曼优化算法（如黎曼梯度下降或黎曼BFGS），在酉矩阵流形上进行约束优化，以搜索干涉仪的最优设置。

Result: 在NOON态和光子催化态的线性光学制备中，改进了一些文献报道的成功概率；此外，优化器比文献中其他方法的运行速度快数个数量级，能够处理涉及更多模式和光子的更大的线性光学问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于闭式黎曼梯度的优化框架为线性光学干涉仪配置优化提供了一种高效且可扩展的方法，为求解更复杂的多模式、多光子线性光学问题铺平了道路。

Abstract: Many applications of linear optics in quantum science involve searching for some optimal configuration of a multiport interferometer. For example, to find heralded state preparations, one can optimize the interferometer to maximize the fidelity and success probability of the state preparation. In this paper, we derive a closed analytical formula for the Riemannian gradient of any differentiable function defined over the interferometer unitaries. We use this gradient in Riemannian optimization algorithms, such as gradient descent or BFGS, to find the optimal setup of the interferometer. As an interesting use case, we search for linear optical state preparations, improving some of the success probabilities reported in the literature for NOON or photon catalysis preparations. Finally, we show that our optimizer is orders of magnitude faster than other methods in the literature, opening the door for solving linear optical problems involving more modes and photons.

</details>


### [142] [Few-Body Decay Dynamics in Colloidal CsPbI3 Quantum-Dot Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07892)
*Emma Daggett,Christian M. Lange,Nicholas Favate,Ankit Kundu,Ishita Agarwal,Arya D. Keni,Adhyyan S. Mansukhani,Christina W. Li,Libai Huang,Jonathan D. Hood*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了CsPbI3钙钛矿量子点从单个发射体到自组装团簇（最多含十个量子点）的辐射动力学，通过光子自相关、闪烁统计和发射亮度估算每个团簇中的发射体数量，发现含两个及以上发射体的团簇呈现双指数衰减，且短寿命随发射体数量减少，确立了钙钛矿量子点自组装团簇作为研究单发射体与系综极限之间集体光学行为涌现的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿量子点兼具明亮发射和自组装成紧密结构的能力，使得从孤立发射体到少量量子点团簇的辐射动力学成为可研究的问题。理解从单发射体到系综极限之间的集体光学行为如何涌现，对于凝聚态光学和量子光学基础研究以及潜在应用都具有重要意义。

Method: 作者对CsPbI3钙钛矿量子点从孤立发射体到含10个点的自组装团簇进行了全面表征：结合光子自相关测量、闪烁统计和发射亮度来估算每个团簇中的发射体数目；利用时间分辨荧光测量研究衰减动力学；采用光子关联傅里叶光谱（PCFS）测量单点发射的相干性。

Result: 与孤立量子点相比，含有两个及以上发射体的团簇表现出双指数衰减，其中短寿命组分随发射体数目增加而变短，并出现额外长寿命组分。光子关联傅里叶光谱显示单点发射远未达到寿命极限（低相干性），但即使在低相干区也观察到依赖于发射体数目的衰减动力学变化。

Conclusion: 这些测量表明发射体数目依赖性衰减动力学在低相干性条件下依然存在，并确立了自组装钙钛矿量子点团簇作为一个研究单发射体与系综极限之间集体光学行为涌现的理想实验平台。

Abstract: Colloidal perovskite quantum dots combine bright emission with the ability to self- assemble into closely spaced structures, enabling radiative dynamics to be studied from isolated emitters to few-dot clusters. Here, we characterize CsPbI3 perovskite quan- tum dots from isolated emitters to self-assembled clusters containing up to ten dots. We estimate the number of emitters in each cluster using a combination of photon autocorrelation, blinking statistics, and emission brightness. Compared with isolated dots, clusters containing two or more emitters exhibit biexponential decay, with a short lifetime that decreases with emitter number and an additional long-lived component. Photon-correlation Fourier spectroscopy shows that the single-dot emission remains far from the lifetime-limited regime. Nevertheless, we observe emitter-number-dependent decay dynamics even in this low-coherence regime. Together, these measurements es- tablish self-assembled perovskite quantum-dot clusters as a platform for studying how collective optical behavior emerges between the single-emitter and ensemble limits

</details>


### [143] [A Novel Steganography Scheme Using Quantum Hilbert Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07894)
*Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT）的量子隐写方案，将经典比特以弱有符号相位扰动形式嵌入量子载体态中，接收端通过二元态判别、块聚合和经典纠错解码来恢复隐藏消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐写术的主要目标是在看似正常的通信消息中传输隐藏信息，而相位域信息隐藏对于量子系统尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 基于量子傅里叶变换构造了有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT）作为酉相位算子，进而开发了一种基于QHT的量子隐写方案，将经典比特作为弱有符号相位扰动嵌入量子覆盖态中；接收方通过二元态判别、块聚合和经典纠错解码恢复隐藏消息。

Result: 提出了一种新的量子隐写方法，利用相位域（QHT算子）实现经典比特在量子载体中的隐蔽嵌入与可靠恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地探索了量子系统中的相位域隐写，通过QHT构造和弱相位扰动策略实现了隐蔽通信，并结合纠错编码保障了解码的可靠性。

Abstract: The main goal of steganography is to transmit hidden messages in legitimate-looking communication messages. Phase-domain information hiding, however, has not been fully explored for quantum systems. This work introduces a finite-dimensional Quantum Hilbert Transform (QHT) as a unitary phase operator based on the Quantum Fourier Transform. Using this construction, we develop a QHT-based quantum steganography scheme that embeds classical bits as weak signed phase perturbations of quantum cover states. Bob recovers the hidden message through binary state discrimination, block aggregation, and classical error-correcting decoding.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07972)
*Sonaldeep Halder,Chayan Patra,Rahul Maitra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-WAVE（量子波函数增强与变分自编码器），一种结合量子硬件采样行列式与CISD行列式、利用β退火变分自编码器生成新主导行列式以迭代扩展基组空间的混合量子-经典方法，在强关联体系中优于纯硬件采样的SQD方法，并在H₂O、N₂、乙烯（52量子比特）和Cr₂（60量子比特）等体系中达到亚毫哈特里或化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQD（基于样本的量子对角化）虽然在量子中心超算中有前景，但在强关联区域中，有限次采样无法覆盖相关行列式空间，导致精度显著下降。需要一种方法突破硬件直接采样的表达极限，利用生成式机器学习扩展波函数基组。

Method: Q-WAVE结合两类种子行列式——通过SqDRIFT Krylov电路采样的硬件行列式和CISD行列式——作为训练数据。使用定制的β退火变分自编码器（VAE）在连续潜空间中学习波函数的主支撑结构，然后迭代地生成超越任何固定激发层次的新的主导行列式，不断扩展示波函数基组直至收敛到变分基态。

Result: 对H₂O和N₂解离过程实现了相对于全组态相互作用（FCI）的亚毫哈特里精度；在52量子比特乙烯体系中达到相对于CCSD(T)的亚毫哈特里精度；在高度相关的60量子比特Cr₂压力测试中，经最终微扰修正达到化学精度。生成的紧凑波函数超越了纯硬件采样所能提取的表达能力。

Conclusion: Q-WAVE证明了生成式机器学习（VAE）可以有效弥合量子硬件有限采样与强关联体系所需的大行列式空间之间的鸿沟，为量子中心超算在处理强关联分子体系时提供了一条兼具准确性和可扩展性的新路径。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) has emerged as a promising route for quantum-centric supercomputing, relying on classical diagonalization of the molecular Hamiltonian within a hardware-sampled determinant subspace. However, its accuracy degrades in strongly correlated regimes where the relevant determinant space exceeds what finite-shot sampling can capture. In this work, we introduce Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders (Q-WAVE), a hybrid method that combines determinants sampled via SqDRIFT Krylov circuits and configuration interaction singles and doubles (CISD) determinants with generative machine learning. Using a custom $β$-annealed variational autoencoder (VAE) model, Q-WAVE iteratively expands this basis toward the variational ground state. The VAE learns the wavefunction's primary support structure from the combined hardware and CISD seed in a continuous latent space, generating new dominant determinants beyond any fixed excitation hierarchy. The resulting compact wavefunction exceeds what can be extracted from raw hardware samples alone. We demonstrate sub-millihartree accuracy compared to full configuration interaction for $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and $\text{N}_2$ dissociation. Finally, we establish Q-WAVE's scalability on a 52-qubit ethylene system (achieving sub-millihartree accuracy versus CCSD(T)) and a highly correlated 60-qubit $\text{Cr}_2$ stress test that attains chemical accuracy upon a final perturbative correction.

</details>


### [145] [Learned Diffractive Optics for Quantum-Optimal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08014)
*Matthew J. Filipovich,Alexander Duplinskii,A. I. Lvovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文应用学习型衍射光学神经网络，在有限光子预算下对相干和衍射受限非相干光场进行态判别与参数估计，其性能显著超越标准测量方法并接近量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理上可实现的测量通常无法达到量子力学设定的光子限制传感基本极限，特别是在视觉传感问题中，由于远距离物体的光场空间结构复杂，构建最优测量方案十分困难，因此亟需一种无需先验最优测量知识即可逼近量子极限的方法。

Method: 将可学习的衍射光学神经网络直接优化于每个任务的品质因数（figure of merit）上，使其作为物理上可实现的光学测量装置，应用于相干光和衍射受限非相干光场的态判别与参数估计任务。

Result: 优化的衍射光学神经网络显著优于标准测量方法，在给定光子数下以及在渐近极限下均逼近量子力学允许的最优性能边界。

Conclusion: 学习型衍射光学提供了一种无需先验最优测量知识的实用途径，可在受限光子预算下实现接近量子极限的光子受限视觉传感，为量子最优测量在复杂光场问题上的物理实现开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics sets the ultimate bounds on photon-limited sensing, yet practical measurements attaining these bounds are known only in special cases. This is particularly the case for visual sensing problems, where the goal is to infer features of a distant object based on the spatial structure of the light field it emits or reflects. Because of the potentially complex structure of such objects and fields, constructing optimal measurements on them is a challenging task. Here, we apply learned diffractive optics to state discrimination and parameter estimation of coherent and diffraction-limited incoherent light fields under a restricted photon budget. Optimized directly on each task's figure of merit, without prior knowledge of the optimal measurement, the physically realizable diffractive optical neural networks substantially outperform standard measurements and approach the quantum limits for a given number of photons as well as in the asymptotic limit.

</details>


### [146] [Sklar's Theorem and Quantum State Reconstruction from All-Context Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08035)
*Yi-Yu Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从Sklar定理（将联合分布的边缘与依赖结构分离）的视角出发，研究局部量子测量产生的Born统计量。对于非直积的双量子比特态，所有局部二元投射测量上下文上的“依赖核”可决定该量子态（至多存在离散的双自旋翻转歧义）。相应的标量优化结合量子总关联可复现全局量子失谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典概率论中Sklar定理（copula理论）的框架引入量子信息领域，为量子关联的结构分析提供新的数学工具。通过分离边缘与依赖结构，可以更深入地理解Born统计量的性质以及量子失谐等关联度量的本质来源。

Method: 借鉴Sklar定理中copula/依赖核的思想，将联合Born概率分布分解为边缘分布与依赖核两部分。在双量子比特系统中，考虑所有局部二元投射测量上下文（即所有可能的局部测量设置），构造相应的依赖核。通过分析依赖核的标量优化问题，并结合量子总关联（quantum total correlation），推导全局量子失谐的表达式。

Result: 证明了在非直积的双量子比特态中，跨越所有局部二元投射测量上下文的依赖核对量子态的刻画是充分的，仅存在至多一个离散的双自旋翻转（double-spin-flip）歧义。依赖核的标量优化与量子总关联相结合能够精确复现全局量子失谐。

Conclusion: Sklar定理的视角为量子Born统计量提供了全新的结构分解，揭示了依赖核在量子态重构和量子关联量化中的核心作用，为量子失谐提供了基于copula理论的另一种等价刻画。

Abstract: Sklar's theorem separates the marginals of a joint distribution from its dependence structure. We apply this viewpoint to Born statistics generated by local quantum measurements. For non-product two-qubit states, the dependence nuclei over all local binary projective measurement contexts determine the state up to at most a discrete double-spin-flip ambiguity. The corresponding scalar optimization, combined with the quantum total correlation, reproduces global quantum discord.

</details>


### [147] [Bottom-Up Design of Quantum Optical Experiments Using Discrete Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08073)
*Isaac L. Huidobro-Meezs,Simón Paiva-Ortega,Rodrigo A. Vargas-Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Grinch框架，一种自底向上、基于奖励驱动的生成式方法，直接从保真度奖励中学习采样光学图结构，无需预训练数据集，用于量子光学实验的逆向设计。该框架在多体纠缠态、图态/簇态及非局域光子门目标上均能生成多个高保真光学图，并发现了渐近解和非局域Toffoli门构造，同时支持硬件约束下的替代CNOT和Toffoli门设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子光学实验设计需要在离散电路拓扑和连续参数空间中搜索，且同一目标态常有多种实现方式。现有基于图的方法通常通过优化稠密图并剪枝得到单一电路，缺乏从奖励直接生成多样化结构的机制，难以探索替代拓扑并融入硬件连接约束。

Method: Grinch采用自底向上的奖励驱动生成框架，从保真度奖励中直接学习采样光学图，不需要预先存在的训练数据集。搜索过程将连接性约束直接纳入其中，可针对硬件限制（如辅助节点间连接受限）调整生成策略。

Result: 在多体纠缠态、图态和簇态以及非局域光子门目标上获得多个高保真光学图。识别出无法由图精确生成的状态的渐近解，发现需要两个辅助节点的非局域Toffoli门构造。在限制辅助节点间连接的硬件约束下，获得了CNOT和Toffoli门的替代构造。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了奖励驱动的量子光学逆向设计方法，能够探索替代电路拓扑并将连接性约束直接纳入搜索过程，为量子光学实验设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Designing quantum optical experiments requires searching over discrete circuit topologies and continuous parameters, often with multiple realizations of the same target state. Graph-based methods commonly address this problem by optimizing a dense graph and pruning it toward a single circuit. We introduce \texttt{Grinch}, a bottom-up reward-driven generative framework that learns to sample optical graphs directly from fidelity-based rewards without relying on a pre-existing training dataset. We demonstrate the framework on multipartite entangled states, graph and cluster states, and nonlocal photonic-gate targets, obtaining multiple high-fidelity optical graphs. We identify asymptotic solutions for states that cannot be generated exactly by graphs, as well as nonlocal Toffoli gate constructions requiring two ancillas. Our work presents hardware-constraint objectives, which lead to alternative constructions of CNOT and Toffoli gates when connections between ancilla nodes are restricted. These findings illustrate a reward-driven approach to quantum optical inverse design, exploring alternative circuit topologies while directly incorporating connectivity constraints into the search process.

</details>


### [148] [Spectral Sign Implication for Quantum Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08099)
*Kenji Tokuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子逻辑中的谱符号蕴涵（spectral sign implication），该运算通过从后件投影减去前件投影所得算子的非负谱投影来定义，在可交换投影上退化为经典实质蕴涵。它满足Hardegree四个最小蕴涵条件、逆否律和假言条件，其值不必属于变元生成的子正交格。在Borel构造的统一类中，满足蕴含性、逆否律和假言条件的运算恰与可测谱分支选择对应；在连续子类中，这三个条件唯一确定谱符号蕴涵。在有限维情形，该运算还是具有与秩成比例先验的Helstrom判别中最优投影测试接收投影中的最大者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子逻辑中蕴涵算子的构造一直是量子基础研究的重要问题。已有的多项式构造（如Sasaki钩）虽然在代数上便捷，但在满足经典蕴涵所需的逻辑条件方面存在局限。作者希望寻找一种能够同时满足Hardegree最小蕴涵条件、逆否律及假言条件的量子蕴涵构造，并揭示其与算子理论（两投影理论）和量子判别（Helstrom判别）之间的深层联系。

Method: 提出谱符号蕴涵（spectral sign implication），通过A→B定义为(B−A)的非负谱投影。利用von Neumann两投影代数理论分析其结构性质，在可交换情形退化为实质蕴涵。建立Borel可测投影构造的统一框架，在此框架内刻画满足蕴含性、逆否律和假言条件的运算与谱分支可测选择的对应关系，并通过连续子类的正则性条件证明唯一性。在有限维中，将运算与该维数下的Helstrom判别（带与秩成比例的先验概率）的最优投影测试的接收投影建立联系。

Result: 谱符号蕴涵满足Hardegree的四个最小蕴涵条件、逆否律及假言条件，但其值不必属于变元生成的ortholattice。在Borel构造类中，蕴含性、逆否律和假言条件恰好刻画了可测谱分支选择；在连续构造子类中，这三个条件唯一确定谱符号蕴涵。在有限维情形，该运算是Helstrom判别二元量子假设测试中接收投影族的最大元（先验比例于秩）。

Conclusion: 谱符号蕴涵为量子逻辑提供了一个满足全部经典逻辑期望性质的蕴涵算子，其在数学结构上与算子理论中两投影的谱分析深刻关联，在信息论上与量子判别中的Helstrom二分最优测试相关联。该工作将量子蕴涵、算子理论和量子假设检验三个领域联系起来，表明量子逻辑中蕴涵的真值可以通过谱投影的几何结构而非代数的多项式操作来自然给出。

Abstract: We study the spectral sign implication for quantum logic, defined by the nonnegative spectral projection of the operator obtained by subtracting the antecedent projection from the consequent projection. The construction agrees with classical material implication on commuting projections and compares arbitrary pairs of projections through the spectral structure of their difference. It satisfies Hardegree's four minimal implicative conditions, his law of contraposition, and a falsity condition. It differs from the standard polynomial implications in that its value need not belong to the ortholattice generated by its arguments. Within a uniform class of Borel constructions for pairs of projections, the operations satisfying entailment, contraposition, and the falsity condition correspond exactly to measurable choices of spectral branch. In the continuous subclass, these three conditions determine the spectral sign implication uniquely. In finite dimensions, the operation is also the largest among the acceptance projections of optimal projective tests for Helstrom discrimination between subspace states with priors proportional to rank. These results connect quantum implication with the operator theory of two projections and binary quantum discrimination.
